6a1(1): @#/ O /can thams cad la phan pa dang /bde ba'i don gyi phyir sems can kyi log par lta ba'i zug rngu rtsa nas 'don pa'i phyir /sems thog ma pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dag byang chub 
6a2(1): sems las phyir mi ldog cing brtan par bya ba'i phyir /nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas thams cad thar phyin pa'i theg pa gcig gis lam la dgod pa'i phyir /byang chub sems dpa' chen po rnams 
6a3(1): myur du mngon par byang chub cing sangs rgyas kyi drin lan bsaba'i phyir sems can thams cad kyis drin lci bar bya ba'i phyir //sems can thams cad kyis drin lci bar bya ba'i phyir/sems can thams

7a2(1): mAN+da ra dang /mAN+da ra chen po yang char bzhin du phab po//'od gzer chen pos snang bar byas shing /rdzu 'phrul gyis 'jig rten gyi khams gang+gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed dag kyang g.yos so//de ma yin pa
